## Northwest Territories Fishery Regulations {#northwest-territories-fishery-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[C.R.C.]{.abbr title="Consolidated Regulations of Canada"}, c. 847
:::

[FISHERIES ACT](/eng/acts/F-14)

Northwest Territories Fishery Regulations
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2005-108,
s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-566316 .Part}

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In these
  Regulations,

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Fisheries
      Act](/eng/acts/F-14); ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the
      [Western Arctic (Inuvialuit) Claims Settlement
      Act](/eng/acts/W-6.7); ([Convention]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[angling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[angling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing with a line to which
      one or more hooks are attached and that is held in the hand or
      attached to a rod that is held in the hand or closely attended;
      ([pêche à la ligne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any portion of the waters
      described in Schedule II; ([secteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[barbless hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[barbless hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a hook that has all
      of its barbs compressed to be in complete contact with the shaft
      of the hook, except those barbs on the shank that are designed
      solely to retain bait; ([hameçon sans ardillon]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is enrolled
      as a beneficiary under the Agreement;
      ([bénéficiaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a registration
      certificate issued pursuant to section 14.1;
      ([certificat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[commercial fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[commercial fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing for sale
      or barter; ([pêche commerciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[commercial licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[commercial licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence
      authorizing a person to engage in commercial fishing; ([permis de
      pêche commerciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[commercial licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[commercial licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person to whom
      a commercial licence has been issued under the Act or these
      Regulations; ([titulaire d'un permis de pêche
      commerciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Department of the
      Environment; ([ministère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[domestic fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[domestic fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing for
      personal use but not for sale or barter; ([pêche
      domestique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[domestic fishing licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[domestic fishing licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence
      authorizing a person to engage in domestic fishing; ([permis de
      pêche domestique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[explosive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[explosive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance or device
      that, when detonated or fired, creates a violent shock wave in
      water; ([explosif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fork length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fork length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the length of a fish
      measured from the tip of the nose to the centre of the fork of the
      tail; ([longueur à la fourche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[game fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[game fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any fish named in Schedule
      I; ([poisson gibier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gill net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gill net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net that catches fish by
      enmeshing them but that does not enclose an area of water; ([filet
      maillant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a single pointed or
      multiple-pointed hook on a common shaft and includes an artificial
      lure that has one or more hooks attached to it;
      ([hameçon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Indian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Indian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an Indian as defined in the
      [Indian Act](/eng/acts/I-5); ([Indien]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Inuk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Inuk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is a direct
      descendant of a person of the race of aborigines commonly known as
      Inuit; ([Inuk]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Inuvialuit Settlement Region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Inuvialuit Settlement Region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in the Agreement; ([région désignée]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of Fisheries
      and Oceans; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[non-resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person other than a
      resident Canadian; ([non-résident]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Northwest Territories resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Northwest Territories resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      resident Canadian who has resided continuously in the Northwest
      Territories for a period of,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of sport fishing, three months
        immediately preceding the day on which the person begins to
        fish; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of commercial fishing, six months
        immediately preceding the day on which the person applies for a
        commercial fishing licence. ([résident des Territoires du
        Nord-Ouest]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fishery officer, fishery
      guardian or a member of the Royal Canadian Mounted Police;
      ([fonctionnaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[permanent resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[permanent resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Revoked,
      SOR/93-59, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[person of mixed blood]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[person of mixed blood]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} mixed Indian and non-Indian blood who is at
        least one-quarter Indian, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} mixed Inuk and non-Inuk blood who is at least
        one-quarter Inuk; ([personne de sang mêlé]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[Regional Director-General]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Regional Director-General]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      Director-General of the Department of Fisheries and Oceans for the
      Central and Arctic Region; ([directeur général
      régional]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Revoked, SOR/91-481, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[resident Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[resident Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is a
      Canadian citizen or a permanent resident; ([résident
      canadien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[snagging]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[snagging]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing by impaling a fish
      on a hook through some part of its body instead of luring the fish
      to take the hook into its mouth; ([casaquer]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[special management area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[special management area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the waters
      described in an item in Schedule VIII; ([secteur de gestion
      spécial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sport fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sport fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fish that is taken for
      pleasure and not for sale or barter; ([poisson de
      sport]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sport fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sport fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing for pleasure
      and not for sale or barter and includes angling, spear fishing and
      dip netting; ([pêche sportive]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sport fishing licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sport fishing licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a sport
      fishing licence set out in Schedule IV that authorizes a person to
      engage in sport fishing; ([permis de pêche
      sportive]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[subsistence usage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[subsistence usage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in paragraph (a) of the definition of that expression in the
      Agreement; ([utilisation à des fins de
      subsistance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Tree River]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Tree River]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the waters of Coronation Gulf within 3 km of a
        point at 67°45′N., 111°56′W.;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all the waters of Port Epworth lying between
        lines of latitude at 67°45′N. and at 67°41′N.;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the waters of the Tree River and its
        tributaries lying between lines of latitude at 67°41′N. and
        67°37′N.; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all other waters that flow directly into any
        waters described in paragraphs (a), (b) or (c). ([Rivière
        Tree]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No Area shall be regarded as including any waters
  described in Schedule III.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-485, s. 1
- SOR/81-119, s. 1
- SOR/91-428, s. 1
- SOR/91-481, s. 1
- SOR/93-59, ss. 1, 17(F)
- SOR/2003-137, s. 2
- SOR/2004-38, s. 1
- SOR/2005-108, s. 3
:::

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-566367 .Part}

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} These
  Regulations apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Canadian fisheries waters in and adjacent to the
    Northwest Territories; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the tidal waters of the provinces of Ontario and
    Manitoba.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply with respect to
  fishing and related activities carried out under the authority of a
  licence issued under the [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-59, s. 2
- SOR/2002-225, s. 1
:::

## [General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-566375 .Part}

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may issue a
licence or certificate set out in column I of an item of Schedule IV on
payment of the appropriate fee, if any, set out in column II of that
item.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-59, s. 2
:::

**[[4.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.1 .sectionLabel}** The licences referred
to in paragraphs (c.1) and (e.1) of item 3 of Schedule IV are not valid
in Nunavut waters.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-114, s. 1](#1126170-1243185){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1126170-1243185.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish except under the authority of a licence issued under
  these Regulations or under the [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332).

- [(2) to (5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Revoked, SOR/93-59, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish in any lake that has been
  stocked with game fish except by angling.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish in any waters set out in Column
  I of an item of Schedule VII for the species of fish set out in Column
  II of that item by the method set out in Column IV of that item during
  the close time set out in Column III of that item.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish in the waters of Fish Creek at
  67°43′42″ north latitude and 136°15′44″ west longitude, or in the
  waters of the Big Fish River at 68°39′48″ north latitude and
  135°52′35″ west longitude, including all their tributaries, except for
  educational or scientific purposes under the authority of a licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-519, s. 1
- SOR/87-508, s. 1
- SOR/93-59, s. 3
- SOR/93-340, s. 1
- SOR/2009-83, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [\[Revoked, SOR/93-59, s.
4\]]{.Repealed}

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall catch, kill, molest or injure fish by using firearms,
  spears, arrows, gaffs, lights, sticks, stones, clubs, snares, dip nets
  or gill nets except under the authority of a licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish by snagging.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), dip nets may be used
  to land fish caught by angling.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-59, s. 5
:::

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** A person who erects or
maintains a structure for fishing upon the ice shall remove the
structure at the direction of an officer.

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** Except with the permission
of an officer, no person shall leave a gill net in the water

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} during the period from May 16th to October 31st
  inclusive, for more than 30 consecutive hours, or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} during the period from November 1st to May 15th
  inclusive, for more than 72 consecutive hours,

without withdrawing the net from the water and removing the fish
therefrom.

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall dispose of dead fish or any remains or offal of fish by
  leaving it in the water or on ice over the water.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} When gurry grounds have been designated or defined by
  an officer, a person who has in his possession dead or decaying fish
  or remnants thereof or other refuse from fishing shall place that
  fish, remnants or refuse in the gurry ground.

## [Stream Crossings]{.HTitleText1} {#h-566402 .Part}

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall construct a temporary crossing over any ice-covered
  stream, unless he

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} uses snow or adds to the thickness of the ice by
    the use of snow or ice, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is authorized by an officer.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Revoked, SOR/93-59, s. 6\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-59, s. 6
:::

## [Logging and Gravel Removal]{.HTitleText1} {#h-566410 .Part}

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where,
  in the opinion of the Minister, the fishery in any waters would be
  harmed by any of the following operations:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} placing logs in or releasing logs into those
    waters,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} driving, towing or booming logs in those waters,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} removing gravel from or displacing gravel in any
    spawning grounds in those waters, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any similar operation,

  he may authorize the placing of signs at appropriate places on the
  shore designating those waters as waters in respect of the fishery of
  which that operation is harmful.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, pursuant to subsection (1), any waters have
  been designated in respect of any operation referred to therein, no
  person shall carry on any such operation in those waters.

## [Commercial Fishing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-566419 .Part}

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall be issued a commercial licence to fish in the waters of
  Lac la Martre unless that person has resided continuously in the
  settlement of Lac la Martre for a period of not less than six months
  immediately preceding the day he applies for that licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall be issued a commercial licence to
  fish in the waters of Kakisa Lake unless that person has resided
  continuously in the settlement of Kakisa Lake for a period of not less
  than six months immediately preceding the day he applies for that
  licence.

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [\[Revoked, SOR/93-59, s.
7\]]{.Repealed}

- **[[14.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall operate, for the purpose of commercial fishing on Great
  Slave Lake, a vessel or vehicle unless he is the holder of a valid
  commercial fishing licence and unless the vessel or vehicle has been
  registered by the Department for that purpose and a registration
  certificate bearing a registration number has been issued in respect
  of that vessel or vehicle.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A Class A certificate may be issued only in respect
  of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a vessel of more than 900 kg gross weight, when
    weighed with its attached mechanical equipment; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a vehicle of more than 900 kg gross weight.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A Class B certificate may be issued in respect of
  vessels or vehicles other than those referred to in subsection (2).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a certificate issued in respect of a
  vessel shall not permit the vessel to be operated in commercial
  fishing, unless the holder of a commercial fishing licence is on
  board.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person shall use a vehicle in fishing under
  subsection (1) unless the registration number issued under these
  Regulations in respect of that vehicle is painted on the vehicle or
  securely affixed to it by some other means and meets the requirements
  set out in subsection (5.1).

- [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} A registration number referred to in subsection (5)
  shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} legible and unobstructed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in solid Arabic numerals of at least 15 cm in
    height that are without ornamentation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} of a colour that contrasts with the background; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} aligned horizontally.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} No person shall work on or fish from a vessel or
  vehicle engaged in commercial fishing on Great Slave Lake unless he is
  the holder of a commercial fishing licence or a helper's licence.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} No person shall dump from a vehicle that is used in
  commercial fishing any fish that has been caught in accordance with
  these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-485, s. 2
- SOR/81-468, s. 1
- SOR/93-59, s. 8
:::

- **[[14.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[registration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in reference to a vessel, registration under the
    [Canada Shipping Act](/eng/acts/S-9); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in reference to a vehicle, registration under the
    Ordinances of the Northwest Territories.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who applies for a certificate in respect
  of a vessel or vehicle shall state

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, the registration or serial number and the
    size of the vessel or vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the owner and operator of the vessel or
    vehicle; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information relating to all auxiliary vessels or
    vehicles to be used in conjunction with the vessel or vehicle.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-485, s. 2
:::

**[[14.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.3 .sectionLabel}** [\[Revoked,
SOR/93-59, s. 9\]]{.Repealed}

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Revoked, SOR/93-59, s.
  10\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every net used in commercial fishing and every gang
  of nets so used shall be marked at each end

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} during open water fishing, by a flag that is not
    less than 45 cm square supported by a buoy; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} during winter fishing, by a stake the top of which
    is at least 1 m above the surface of the water or ice.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-485, s. 3
- SOR/81-468, s. 2
- SOR/93-59, s. 10
:::

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** The web of a gill net
used for commercial fishing shall not be hung on any portion of the cork
line of the net at a ratio greater than 2 m of web to 1 m of cork line
and no additional nets shall be hung on, added to or inserted in the web
of the net to increase that ratio at any place in the net.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-468, s. 3
:::

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish for commercial purposes except in waters set out in
  Schedule V.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for commercial purposes in
  waters set out in Schedule III.

<!-- -->

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish in any waters set out in Column I of an item of
  Schedule V for any species of fish set out in Column II of that item,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless the person uses a gill net having a mesh
    size not less than that set out in Column III of that item, where
    applicable; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} during the closed season set out in Column IV of
    that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1) or any licence
  condition, the mesh size of a net shall be determined by measuring the
  distance between the extreme angles of a single mesh inside and
  between the knots after the twine has been immersed in water for at
  least 30 minutes and extended until straight without stretching or
  straining the twine or slipping a knot.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The measurement referred to in subsection (2) shall
  be made by means of a measuring device certified under the [Weights
  and Measures Act](/eng/acts/W-6).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/98-388, s. 1
:::

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish in the waters set out in column I of an item of
  Schedule V after the time specified in a notice issued by the Regional
  Director-General or a fishery officer stating that the quota set out
  in column V of that item has been or is about to be reached.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice referred to in subsection (1) shall be
  brought to the attention of the persons affected or likely to be
  affected by it by one or more of the methods set out in subsection
  7(1) of the [Fishery (General)
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-369, s. 1
- SOR/81-119, s. 4
- SOR/93-59, s. 11
:::

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
calculating the quotas for the water areas of Great Slave Lake, each
annual quota shall commence on November 1st and end on October 31st next
following.

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** No person shall set a net
or running line in any waters at any time during the closed season for
those waters.

## [Native and Domestic Fishing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-566480 .Part}

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsections 5(1) and
  7(1), an Indian, Inuk or person of mixed blood may fish without a
  licence by angling or by means of gill nets, set lines, spears, snares
  or dip nets, for food for himself, his family or his dogs.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall give any fish caught under the
  authority of subsection (1) to any person other than an Indian, Inuk
  or person of mixed blood.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person, other than an Indian, Inuk or person of
  mixed blood, shall accept or be in possession of any fish caught under
  the authority of subsection (1).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Revoked, SOR/93-59, s. 12\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-119, s. 5
- SOR/93-59, ss. 12, 17(F)
:::

- **[[22.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsections 5(1) and
  7(1), a beneficiary may fish for subsistence usage within the
  Inuvialuit Settlement Region without a licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection 5(4) and subject to
  section 12 of the Agreement, a beneficiary may sell to or trade or
  barter with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} other beneficiaries, fish or any portions thereof
    taken for subsistence usage within the Inuvialuit Settlement Region;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other person, the non-edible by-products of
    fish taken from the Inuvialuit Settlement Region for personal use.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person other than a beneficiary shall possess any
  edible portions of fish taken for subsistence usage from the
  Inuvialuit Settlement Region.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-481, s. 2
:::

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** A domestic fishing
licence may be issued to a person who

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} has resided in the Northwest Territories for at least
  two years;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} needs fish for food for himself or his family; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} needs fish for food for his dogs.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-485, s. 4
- SOR/93-59, s. 13
:::

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [\[Revoked, SOR/93-59, s.
13\]]{.Repealed}

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** Every person who is the
holder of a domestic fishing licence shall, upon request by an officer,
state the area or areas in which he fished and the species and number of
kilograms of fish taken by him during the term of the licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-468, s. 4
:::

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** An officer may mark for
identification purposes any fish caught by any person in domestic
fishing.

## [Sport Fishing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-566507 .Part}

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection 5(1) and
  subject to subsection (2), a person may engage in sport fishing
  without a sport fishing licence if the person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Northwest Territories resident or a resident
    Canadian under the age of 16 years or 65 years of age or over; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a non-resident under the age of 16 years who is
    accompanied by a person who holds a sport fishing licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in sport fishing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in a special management area without

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a sport fishing licence, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a special management area fishing licence which
      has been validated for that special management area; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the Inuvialuit Settlement Region without a sport
    fishing licence which has been validated for the Inuvialuit
    Settlement Region.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-481, s. 3
:::

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 38, no person
  angling or spear-fishing in the waters set out in column II of an item
  of Schedule VI shall catch and retain in any one day more fish of a
  species set out in column I of that item than the number set out in
  column III of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Revoked, SOR/93-59, s. 14\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Revoked, SOR/87-508, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-485, s. 5
- SOR/81-119, s. 6
- SOR/87-508, s. 2
- SOR/91-428, s. 2
- SOR/93-59, s. 14
:::

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 38, no person
  shall have in their possession at any time more fish of a species set
  out in column I of Schedule VI taken from the waters set out in column
  II than the number set out in column IV.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1) and subsection
  28(1), where a fish has been filleted, two fillets shall be regarded
  as one fish.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 38(3), no person shall have in
  his possession any fish taken by sport fishing at any place other than
  his permanent residence or transport any such fish unless the fish are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whole;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} eviscerated; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} filleted with the skin attached.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (3)(c), fillets shall
  be separated before being frozen so that the number of fish can be
  readily determined.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person shall place, or permit to be placed, in a
  storage facility any fish taken by sport fishing unless such fish are
  identified by the name and the sport fishing licence number of the
  person who caught them.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-485, s. 6
- SOR/91-428, s. 3
- SOR/2003-137, s. 3
:::

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** No person shall engage in
sport fishing while employed as a guide by a person engaged in fishing.

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, in any year, sportfish in the Tree River after notice is
  given by a fishery officer that 700 arctic char have been taken from
  that river in that year.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person fishing in the waters of the Tree River
  shall take away or cause to be taken away from those waters more than
  two arctic char during any fishing trip.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to an Indian,
  Inuk or person of mixed blood.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A notice referred to in subsection (1) shall be
  brought to the attention of the persons affected or likely to be
  affected by it by one or more of the methods set out in subsection
  7(1) of the [Fishery (General)
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-119, s. 7
- SOR/91-428, s. 4
- SOR/93-59, s. 15
:::

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall engage in spear fishing except while swimming and under
  authority of a sport fishing licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2003-137, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} All spears used in spear fishing shall be fastened to
  the spear gun or to the person operating the spear by a line of
  adequate strength to withstand without breaking any strain that is
  likely to be placed on the line while the gun is being operated.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The shaft of every spear referred to in
  subsection (3) shall be fastened to the spear gun or the person using
  the spear by a line not exceeding 5 m in length.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person shall use, as a propellant in a spear gun,
  any explosive charge or any gas contained in a bottle or cartridge.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-468, s. 5
- SOR/2003-137, s. 4
:::

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** No person shall use or be
in possession of a gaff while sport fishing.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-369, s. 2
:::

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection 7(1) but
  subject to subsection (2), no person shall use a dip net having a hoop
  width greater than 1 m in fishing for cisco and suckers.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any fish of a species other than ciscos and suckers
  taken by means of a dip net shall be returned to the water
  immediately.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-485, s. 7
- SOR/81-468, s. 6
- SOR/93-59, s. 17(F)
:::

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, while
sport fishing,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} fish in open water with more than a single line or
  rod and line;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} fish through the ice with more than two lines or two
  rods with lines attached;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} have more than two hooks attached to a line; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} leave any line with hooks attached unattended.

**[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** No person shall engage in
sport fishing in ice-covered water if he is more than 50 m from his
line.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-468, s. 7
:::

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, when
sport fishing, use any live fish for bait.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-485, s. 8
:::

- **[[37.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person engaged in angling shall use or possess a hook that is attached
  to the end of a fishing line if the hook is not a barbless hook.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to angling in Nunavut
  waters.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-38, s. 2
- SOR/2005-108, s. 4
:::

## [Size Limits]{.HTitleText1} {#h-566576 .Part}

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall have in his possession

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any arctic grayling that is less than 36 cm fork
    length, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} more than one northern pike that is more than 90 cm
    fork length

  taken from that portion of the Mackenzie River and tributary streams
  lying between 116° west longitude and 118° west longitude.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} possess more than one lake trout that is longer
    than 70 cm fork length taken from Stark Lake or Great Slave Lake,
    including all tributary streams from their mouths to 1 km upstream;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} remove from the area referred to in paragraph (a)
    more than two lake trout.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, on or adjacent to the waters
  described in subsection (1), have in his possession any Arctic
  grayling from which the head and tail have been removed unless the
  length of any such fish has been checked by a fishery officer before
  the head and tail were removed.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} possess more than two lake trout taken from the
    waters of the special management area described in item 1 of
    Schedule VIII; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} remove from the area referred to in paragraph (a)
    more than one lake trout.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-485, s. 9
- SOR/81-468, s. 8
- SOR/91-481, s. 4
- SOR/2003-137, s. 5
:::

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [\[Revoked, SOR/93-59, s.
16\]]{.Repealed}

## [SCHEDULE II]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 2)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-566603 .scheduleLabel}
:::

### [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Areas of Great Slave Lake]{.HTitleText1} {#part-iareas-of-great-slave-lake .SchedHeadL1}

#### [Area I (West)]{.HTitleText2} {#area-i-west .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of Great Slave Lake lying

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} east of a line drawn from Point Desmarais in 61°00′48
  north latitude and 116°28′12 west longitude to a point on Big Island
  in 61°05′18 north latitude and 116°30′06 west longitude; THENCE to a
  point on Big Island in 61°11′06 north latitude and 116°39′24 west
  longitude; and THENCE to a point in 61°12′26 north latitude and
  116°41′06 west longitude; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} west of a line drawn from Point de Roche to Slave
  Point.

#### [Area I (East)]{.HTitleText2} {#area-i-east .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of Great Slave Lake lying east of a line drawn from Point
de Roche to Slave Point and west of a line drawn from Jones Point in
61°27′ north latitude and 115°43′ west longitude to Sulphur Point in
60°56′ north latitude and 114°49′ west longitude.

#### [Area II]{.HTitleText2} {#area-ii .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of Great Slave Lake lying north and east of Area I (East)
and south and west of a line drawn from the mouth of the north branch of
the Jean River in 61°22′ north latitude and 113°33′ west longitude to
the most northerly tip of Hardisty Island in 61°45′ north latitude and
114°37′ west longitude and THENCE to a fishing boundary marker on the
mainland, and north of a straight line drawn between the mouth of the
north branch of the Jean River and Slave Point to where the said line
intersects the east boundary of Area I (East).

#### [Area III]{.HTitleText2} {#area-iii .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of Great Slave Lake lying east of Area I (East) and south
of Area II.

#### [Area IV]{.HTitleText2} {#area-iv .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of Great Slave Lake lying between Area II and Water Area V.

#### [Area V]{.HTitleText2} {#area-v .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of Great Slave Lake lying east of the meridian of 113°30′00
west longitude and west of a line drawn from a point on the mainland in
62°04′43 north latitude 112°30′00 west longitude to a point on the north
shore of Blanchette Island in 62°01′45 north latitude and 112°30′00″
west longitude, THENCE along the north shore of Blanchette Island to the
most easterly point of Blanchette Island, THENCE to the most westerly
point of Et Then Island, THENCE along the south shore of Et Then Island,
THENCE along the south shore of Et Then Island to the most easterly
point of Et Then Island THENCE due east to the mainland.

#### [Area VI]{.HTitleText2} {#area-vi .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of Great Slave Lake lying east and north of Area V.

### [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Areas of the Mackenzie River Delta and Lower Mackenzie River]{.HTitleText1} {#part-iiareas-of-the-mackenzie-river-delta-and-lower-mackenzie-river .SchedHeadL1}

#### [Area I]{.HTitleText2} {#area-i .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of the Mackenzie Delta bounded

- [(1)]{.lawlabel} in the east by the west bank of the Middle Channel
  from the point at 67°41.5′ north latitude, 134°32.8′ west longitude to
  the point on the west bank of the Middle Channel at 68°13′ north
  latitude, 134°28′ west longitude;

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} in the north by the parallel of latitude at 68°13′
  north from the point on the west bank of the Middle Channel at 134°28′
  west longitude to the point on the west bank of the Husky Channel at
  135°06′ west longitude;

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} in the west by the west bank of the Husky Channel
  from the point at 68°13′ north latitude, 135°06′ west longitude to the
  point where the Husky Channel meets the Peel River at 67°37′ north
  latitude, 134°52′ west longitude; and

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} in the south by the south bank of the Peel River from
  the point at 67°37′ north latitude, 134°52′ west longitude to the
  point on the west bank of the Middle Channel at 67°41.5′ north
  latitude, 134°32.8′ west longitude.

#### [Area II]{.HTitleText2} {#area-ii-1 .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of the Mackenzie Delta bounded

- [(1)]{.lawlabel} in the east by the east bank of the East Channel from
  the point at Point Separation at 67°37′ north latitude, 134°05′ west
  longitude to the point at 68°13′ north latitude, 133°47′ west
  longitude;

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} in the north by the parallel of latitude at 68°13′
  north from the point on the east bank of the East Channel at 133°47′
  west longitude to the point on the west bank of the Middle Channel at
  134°28′ west longitude;

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} in the west by the west bank of the Middle Channel
  from the point at 68°13′ north latitude, 134°28′ west longitude to the
  point opposite Point Separation at 67°37′ north latitude, 134°10′ west
  longitude; and

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} in the south by the parallel of latitude extending
  across the Mackenzie River at 67°37′ north from the point at 134°10′
  west longitude to the point at Point Separation at 134°05′ west
  longitude.

#### [Area III]{.HTitleText2} {#area-iii-1 .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of the Mackenzie Delta bounded

- [(1)]{.lawlabel} in the east by the west bank of the Middle Channel
  from the point at 68°13′ north latitude, 134°28′ west longitude to the
  junction of the Raymond Channel and the Middle Channel at 68°15.5′
  north latitude, 134°25′ west longitude, then by the west bank of the
  Raymond Channel to the junction of the Raymond Channel and Napoiak
  Channel at 68°26′ north latitude, 134°24′ west longitude, then by the
  west bank of the Napoiak Channel to the point at the mouth of the
  Napoiak Channel at Shallow Bay at 68°41′ north latitude, 135°14′ west
  longitude, then by a line through Shallow Bay to the point at 68°59.5′
  north latitude, 136°27′ west longitude;

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} in the west by the meridian at 136°27′ west longitude
  from the point at 68°59.5′ north latitude to the Moose Channel at
  68°44′ north latitude; then by the south bank of the Moose Channel to
  the point at the junction of the Moose Channel and the West Channel at
  68°39′ north latitude, 135°45′ west longitude, then by the west bank
  of the West Channel to the community of Aklavik at 68°13′ north
  latitude, 135°00′ west longitude, then by the west bank of the Husky
  Channel to the point at 68°13′ north latitude, 135°06′ west longitude;
  and

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} in the south by the parallel of latitude at 68°13′
  north from the point on the west bank of the Husky Channel at 135°06′
  west longitude to the point on the west bank of the Middle Channel at
  134°28′ west longitude.

#### [Area IV]{.HTitleText2} {#area-iv-1 .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of the Mackenzie Delta bounded

- [(1)]{.lawlabel} in the east by the east bank of the East Channel from
  the point at 68°13′ north latitude, 133°47′ west longitude to the
  Beaufort Sea, then by the coast of the Beaufort Sea to the point at
  69°38′ north latitude, 132°54′ west longitude, then by the meridian at
  132°54′ west longitude to the extent of the territorial sea of Canada
  as defined in subsection 3(1) of the Territorial Sea and Fishing Zones
  Act;

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} in the north by the extent of the territorial sea of
  Canada as defined in subsection 3(1) of the Territorial Sea and
  Fishing Zones Act from the meridian at 132°54′ west longitude to the
  meridian at 136°27′ west longitude;

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} in the west by the meridian at 136°27′ west longitude
  from the extent of the territorial sea of Canada as defined in
  subsection 3(1) of the Territorial Sea and Fishing Zones Act to the
  point at 68°59.5′ north latitude, then by a line through Shallow Bay
  to the point at 68°41′ north latitude, 135°14′ west longitude at the
  mouth of the Napoiak Channel, then by the west bank of the Napoiak
  Channel to the junction of the Napoiak Channel and the Raymond Channel
  at 68°26′ north latitude, 134°24′ west longitude, then by the west
  bank of the Raymond Channel to the junction of the Raymond Channel and
  the Middle Channel at 68°15.5′ north latitude, 134°25′ west longitude,
  then by the west bank of the Middle Channel to the point at 68°13′
  north latitude, 134°28′ west longitude; and

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} in the south by the parallel of latitude at 68°13′
  north from the point on the west bank of the Middle Channel at 134°28′
  west longitude to the point on the east bank of the East Channel at
  133°47′ west longitude.

#### [Area V]{.HTitleText2} {#area-v-1 .SchedHeadL2}

- [1]{.lawlabel} That portion of the Mackenzie River bounded

  - [(1)]{.lawlabel} in the north by the parallel of latitude across the
    River at Point Separation at 67°37′ north latitude from the point at
    134°05′ west longitude to the point at 134°10′ west longitude; and

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} in the south by the meridian of longitude at
    128°49′ west extending across the River at the confluence of the
    Mountain River at 65°41′ north latitude.

- [2]{.lawlabel} Also that portion of the Arctic Red River from its
  confluence with the Mackenzie River to the point 16 km upstream at
  67°19′ north latitude, 133°42′ west longitude.

#### [Area VI]{.HTitleText2} {#area-vi-1 .SchedHeadL2}

That portion of the Mackenzie River Delta bounded:

- [(1)]{.lawlabel} in the east by a straight line joining a point at
  68°40′ north latitude and 135°45′ west longitude to a point at 68°53′
  north latitude and 135°45′ west longitude,

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} in the north by a straight line joining a point at
  68°53′ north latitude and 135°45′ west longitude to a point at 68°53′
  north latitude and 136°27′ west longitude,

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} in the west by a straight line joining a point at
  68°53′ north latitude and 136°27′ west longitude to a point at 68°44′
  north latitude and 136°27′ west longitude, and

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} in the south by a straight line joining a point at
  68°44′ north latitude and 136°27′ west longitude to a point at
  68°38.5′ north latitude and 136°03′ west longitude, on the north bank
  of the Big Fish River and THENCE by the north bank of the Big Fish
  River from that point at 68°38.5′ north latitude and 136°03′ west
  longitude to the junction of the Big Fish River and Little Moose
  Channel and THENCE by a straight line across the mouth of the Big Fish
  River from the junction of the Big Fish River and Little Moose Channel
  to a point at 68°40′ north latitude and 135°52′ west longitude and
  THENCE by a straight line joining that point at 68°40′ north latitude
  and 135°52′ west longitude to a point at 68°40′ north latitude and
  135°45′ west longitude.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-720, s. 1

## [SCHEDULE III]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 2 and 17)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-566659 .scheduleLabel}
:::

### [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Waters Areas of Great Slave Lake Not Included in Areas Described in Schedule II]{.HTitleText1} {#part-iwaters-areas-of-great-slave-lake-not-included-in-areas-described-in-schedule-ii .SchedHeadL1}

- [1]{.lawlabel} Waters within 8 km of the shore between a line drawn
  360° true from Point de Roche in 60°53′ north latitude and 116°07′
  west longitude and a line drawn 360° true from Fish Point in 60°50′
  north latitude and 115°22′ west true longitude.

- [2]{.lawlabel} Waters within

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 16 km of the settlement of Resolution; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 8 km of the settlement of Trout Rock in 62°27′
    north latitude and 114°58′ west longitude.

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} \[Repealed, SOR/2004-38, s. 5\]

- [3]{.lawlabel} Waters lying north and west of a line drawn across the
  North Arm from Whitebeach Point in 62°28′ north latitude and 115°16′
  west longitude, to a point on the mainland at 62°31′ north latitude
  and 115°13′ west longitude.

- [4]{.lawlabel} Waters of Yellowknife Bay north of a line drawn from
  the northernmost tip of Ruth Island (62°19′ north latitude and 114°14′
  west longitude) in a westerly direction to the northernmost tip of
  Inner Island (62°18′ north latitude and 114°18′ west longitude) THENCE
  in a westerly direction to an island at 62°19′ north latitude and
  114°27′ west longitude and THENCE in a northwesterly direction to a
  point on the mainland at 62°21′ north latitude and 114°30′ west
  longitude.

- [5]{.lawlabel} All of the waters of Great Slave Lake lying east and
  north of Area V including the waters of Stark Lake and the river
  connecting Stark Lake and Great Slave Lake.

### [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Water Areas Closed to Commercial Fishing]{.HTitleText1} {#part-iiwater-areas-closed-to-commercial-fishing .SchedHeadL1}

- [1]{.lawlabel} Waters of Rankin Inlet within 8 km of the mouth of the
  Diana River at 62°50′ north latitude and 92°24′ west longitude.

- [2]{.lawlabel} Waters of Prairie Bay in Rankin Inlet within 8 km of
  the mouth of the Meliadine River at 62°52′ north latitude and 92°06
  west longitude.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-468, s. 10
- SOR/83-503, s. 1
- SOR/2004-38, s. 5
:::
